# CLEAN-INTEL2-1115

Aggressive cleanup of intel2 hub. Filesystems: scratch2 (nvme0n1) and / == scratch3 (nvme1n1p2, shared). The 10:35-10:38 ENOSPC on / + scratch3 (28G free, 94%); fixed by removing 3 clones + 1 worktree under /home/ubuntu/wt (194 GB).

Kept for later pass: autopilot/closed current-plan (7073a6b4a5) + ref d records; git units tl-qcphysd-push/r 57G, rom-viamap-assy 49G (HEAD not on origin), wt-oldplan-1115, wt-kvgenbound-1115, tl-leaf42setup-wt, fix-selfcont/wt, phyt1/wt, core-bksel-1115/c2 (recent files); s81-types-7/repo 34G + codex-qc-cbn-qualified-1106/repo + flow-canary/ab/sources/* (unpushed refs); kw44, die-qc18-1028, die-hbm-10 (symlink targets); i2root-moved-1114/dkv80rf/repo (referenced); ~/wt/kitops6-1114 33G (recent); ~/rehost1/repo 30G, ~/scratch-root/claude 77G (epyc2 refs) unreviewed.
